## Decision Recorded
**Timestamp**: 2026-11-06T06:52:13Z
**Event**: DECISION_RECORDED
**Stage**: nfr-requirements
**Decision**: Anything to add for next time?
**Options**: Q1–Q6=A：D1 p95＜60s／90s 硬逾時；非 LLM p95＜2s／zip＜5s；Checkov；≤3 槽／16MiB；best-effort memory；結構化 log 無 metrics。,Q1–Q5=A：busy≥90s、客戶端不傾印密文、a11y AA 目標、既有 React stack、無強制 RUM。,NFR5 a11y 標 N/A（歸 U2）；D2 選 Checkov 而非雙工具以控本輪範圍。,UI unit 不產 scale／reliability／observability 檔；NFR2／NFR4 標 N/A 歸 U1。,Nothing to add,Add a note
